Terminal-ACK settlement correction (parent3a3d0c7b): connectGrpc tracks only outstanding ACK promises, removes settled entries and awaits them before deciding delivery success and before cancellation-listener cleanup on native failure. Previously the final ACK rejection could arrive after successful return/final history persistence and be lost. Existing first-error propagation and native-error precedence remain. Inline Bun runs actual connectGrpc with controlled delayed IPC: success waits for ACK, delayed ACK failure rejects with exact cause, native failure waits for ACK cleanup and preserves native cause. Listener remains attached until settlement. Final compiler0errors0warnings followed by build0. Broader ACK/cancellation/wire/UI/provider/platform and PLAN/PARITY remain open. /compact unavailable; handoff recorded. Topic commit follows.
Delivery-error correction (parent36f754e5): grpc.js now tracks failure independently from the thrown value, propagating null/false/0/empty-string callback exceptions and retaining the first delivery failure across subsequent callbacks/ACK failures. Delayed streaming-send correction (parent cfecebaf): sendGrpc captures the originating response before native IPC. Accepted messages append to that response if still displayed or retained in history, even after selection changes; removed calls receive no late event. If terminal persistence or selection retirement has already happened, history quotas are reapplied and a serialized persistence request records the late acceptance. Active open sends retain existing persistence cadence. Inline Bun execution of the actual sendGrpc function with controlled IPC dependencies passes open, terminal-before-acceptance, history-switch and removed-response cases, exact large-integer JSON preservation, no writes to selected unrelated history and completion cleanup. Source parent c9e1c981. Unary and server-streaming calls previously queued their rendered request natively but never appended a sent event; only client-streaming sends populated sent-message history. executeGrpc now retains the exact rendered JSON when the native connected event arrives, guarded by the connecting phase to avoid duplicate connected delivery. Native parses/queues this body before connection setup; failed parsing/connection does not invent sent history. Existing retention, Sent tabs and final remember/persist paths consume the event. ## Current legacy JSON numeric overflow compatibility

<a id="current-native-grpc-overflow"></a>

Corrected legacy JSON parsing to retain JavaScript Number behavior for valid overflow/underflow literals. Previously serde_json::Value rejected1e400 before protobuf.js-style coercion. The legacy-only Json tree uses validated borrowed RawValue captures and f64 numeric nodes; no global arbitrary_precision/JSON configuration or dependency change. ProtoJSON stays separate. Duplicate keys remain last-wins, object ordering retains BTreeMap behavior, negativezero is preserved, syntax validation remains serde_json-backed,127nested containers pass/128refuse. Existing20MiB input/encoded bounds and64message-depth guard remain. Actual parser8vectors RED101/GREEN0 and full23Rust library tests pass, including syntax/duplicate/nesting/array checks and prior numeric/Long/Base64/output regressions. Evidence artifacts/grpc-overflow-json-reference.json and grpc-overflow-json-red/green-all-v2 logs plus exit files.

Eight Windows native-hidden cases cover +/-1e400,+/-1e309,+/-1e308,+/-1e-400 across optional double/int32/int64 and string fields. Real mounted editor typing/Format/JSON mode flip preserve every authored exponent lexeme through reload. Independent wire literals match pinned protobuf.js7.2.4/Long5.3.2: Infinity/-Infinity IEEE754, int32zero, signedLong saturation, explicit optional selectors/zero and negativezero wire bit. Responses retain null for nonfinite doubles, finite exponent/zero values, exact Long decimal strings and JavaScript String conversions. Complete history/body/reload and16actual JSON exports before/after reload pass; Manage/Delete prevents resurrection, unrelated histories stay exact. Key-order equivalence remains open and is not normalized or claimed by these cases.

## Current native Base64 refusal and recovery

<a id="current-native-base64-refusal"></a>

## Current native gRPC JSON refusal and repair

<a id="current-native-grpc-json-refusal"></a>

Four bounded Windows native-hidden cases verify malformed JSON in legacy/protoJson and a valid message with an unrecognized field in each mode. Legacy accepts the unknown field without rewriting the authored request; the wire/response omit it according to its explicit legacy contract. ProtoJSON refuses the unknown field in its native parser. Both modes reject malformed JSON during renderer preflight with a visible SyntaxError and no fabricated call history.

Three refusals show a diagnostic with zero additional observed fixture streams/sessions/sockets. Renderer malformed-JSON refusal creates no call history before/after reload and retains the authored body. Native unknown-field refusal creates one durable error entry with no sent/received payload; reload preserves it exactly and does not dispatch. Actual mounted/focused CodeMirror keyboard repair followed by explicit Load methods/Send produces a successful history, status0 and the exact independently authored19byte int64/bytes frame. Native error and all success histories survive reload unchanged; unrelated histories remain exact. Actual Manage/Delete removes all owned history and cannot resurrect it. This is owned-peer evidence, not a universal absence-of-network-side-effects claim.

Initial receipt1792074063820 fails a wrongly authored requirement that renderer-invalid JSON should create native failed-call history: actual SyntaxError/zero fixture IO/no history is correct. Host29364 exits0/native-hidden, profile1792074063493 restores original f0b51/d8b032, failed full closure is preserved. Corrected test separates refusal boundaries without fabricating history or relaxing peer-IO/keyboard-repair/reload/export/deletion assertions. src/lib/grpc-model.js grpcBody validates JSON before response construction; archived main/ipc/grpc.ts parses at209 before makeUnaryRequest/grpc.start and replies grpc.error at263. Host58900 exits0/native-hidden/profile1792074785118 restores exactly and failed closure passes. Matcher is corrected to the actual diagnostic, retaining zero-peer-IO/history/repair assertions and requiring the diagnostic to clear after success. No application change is claimed.
